## Source: packages/explorEDA/src/components/settings/ThemeSettingsPanel.tsx

~~~~~~~~text
import { useRef } from "react";
import { RotateCcw } from "lucide-react";
import { useDataLayer } from "@/providers/DataLayerProvider";
import {
  WORKSPACE_THEMES,
  isWorkspaceThemeId,
  resolveThemeId,
} from "@/lib/themes";
import {
  listScaleOverrides,
  listStyleOverrides,
  resetChartStyle,
  resetScaleToTheme,
  type StyleOverride,
} from "@/lib/styleOverrides";
import { getChartTitle } from "../charts/chartAccessibility";
import { useThemeTypography } from "../charts/chartTypography";
import { focusChartInContainer } from "../chartFocus";
import { Button } from "../ui/button";
import { ActionTooltip } from "../ui/tooltip";
import { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem } from "../ui/toggle-group";

function formatValue(override: Pick<StyleOverride, "unit">, value: number) {
  return override.unit === "px" ? `${value} px` : String(value);
}

/**
 * Picks the workspace theme, and lists the charts that deliberately differ
 * from it. Charts restyle as soon as a theme is chosen, and the choice saves
 * with the analysis.
 */
export function ThemeSettingsPanel() {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const themeId = resolveThemeId(useDataLayer((state) => state.theme));
  const setTheme = useDataLayer((state) => state.setTheme);
  const charts = useDataLayer((state) => state.charts);
  const updateChart = useDataLayer((state) => state.updateChart);
  const getFieldLabel = useDataLayer((state) => state.getFieldLabel);
  // The panel sits in the themed workspace, so it reads the theme's values.
  const { typography } = useThemeTypography(ref, themeId);
  const overridden = listStyleOverrides(charts);
  const colorScales = useDataLayer((state) => state.colorScales);
  const updateColorScale = useDataLayer((state) => state.updateColorScale);
  const scaleOverrides = listScaleOverrides(colorScales);
  const total = overridden.length + scaleOverrides.length;

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="eda-theme-settings">
      <ToggleGroup
        type="single"
        value={themeId}
        onValueChange={(value) => {
          if (isWorkspaceThemeId(value)) setTheme(value);
        }}
        aria-label="Workspace theme"
        className="eda-theme-options"
      >
        {WORKSPACE_THEMES.map((theme) => (
          <ActionTooltip key={theme.id} content={theme.description}>
            <ToggleGroupItem
              value={theme.id}
              aria-label={theme.name}
              className="eda-theme-option"
            >
              <span
                className="eda-theme-swatch"
                data-eda-theme={theme.id}
                data-headline={theme.headline}
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <span className="eda-theme-swatch-title">Aa</span>
                <span className="eda-theme-swatch-rule" />
              </span>
              <span className="eda-theme-name">{theme.name}</span>
            </ToggleGroupItem>
          </ActionTooltip>
        ))}
      </ToggleGroup>

      <section className="eda-setting-section" aria-labelledby="eda-overrides">
        <h5 id="eda-overrides">
          Overrides
          <span className="eda-override-count">{total}</span>
        </h5>
        {total === 0 ? (
          <p className="eda-setting-note">
            Every chart follows the{" "}
            {WORKSPACE_THEMES.find((t) => t.id === themeId)!.name} theme.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="eda-override-list">
            {overridden.map(({ chart, overrides }) => {
              const title = getChartTitle(chart, getFieldLabel);
              return (
                <li key={chart.id}>
                  <div className="eda-override-chart">
                    <ActionTooltip content="Show this chart in the workspace">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="eda-override-title"
                        onClick={() =>
                          focusChartInContainer(
                            ref.current?.closest(".eda-workspace") ?? null,
                            chart.id
                          )
                        }
                      >
                        {title}
                      </button>
                    </ActionTooltip>
                    <Button
                      variant="ghost"
                      size="icon"
                      aria-label={`Reset ${title} to the theme`}
                      tooltip="Return all of this chart's type to the theme. Fields, filters, and layout stay."
                      onClick={() =>
                        updateChart(chart.id, resetChartStyle(chart))
                      }
                    >
                      <RotateCcw />
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                  <dl>
                    {overrides.map((override) => (
                      <div key={override.key} className="eda-override-row">
                        <dt>{override.label}</dt>
                        <dd>
                          <b>{formatValue(override, override.value)}</b>
                          <span>
                            theme{" "}
                            {formatValue(
                              override,
                              typography[override.themeKey]
                            )}
                          </span>
                          <Button
                            variant="ghost"
                            size="icon"
                            aria-label={`Reset ${override.label} of ${title}`}
                            tooltip={`Return ${override.label.toLowerCase()} to the theme`}
                            onClick={() =>
                              updateChart(chart.id, override.reset)
                            }
                          >
                            <RotateCcw />
                          </Button>
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </dl>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        )}
        {scaleOverrides.length > 0 && (
          <ul className="eda-override-list" aria-label="Color scales">
            {scaleOverrides.map(({ scale, paletteName, handPicked }) => (
              <li key={scale.id}>
                <div className="eda-override-chart">
                  <span className="eda-override-title">{scale.name}</span>
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    size="icon"
                    aria-label={`Return ${scale.name} to the theme palette`}
                    tooltip="Use the theme's palette. Each category keeps its place, and hand-picked colors are cleared."
                    onClick={() =>
                      updateColorScale(scale.id, resetScaleToTheme(scale))
                    }
                  >
                    <RotateCcw />
                  </Button>
                </div>
                <dl>
                  {paletteName && (
                    <div className="eda-override-row">
                      <dt>Palette</dt>
                      <dd>
                        <b>{paletteName}</b>
                        <span>theme palette instead</span>
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {handPicked > 0 && (
                    <div className="eda-override-row">
                      <dt>Hand-picked</dt>
                      <dd>
                        <b>
                          {handPicked} {handPicked === 1 ? "color" : "colors"}
                        </b>
                        <span>stay the same in every theme</span>
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </dl>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
~~~~~~~~

## Source: packages/explorEDA/src/components/settings/annotations/AnnotationFields.tsx

~~~~~~~~text
import type { IdType } from "@/providers/DataLayerProvider";
import type { AnnotationContext } from "@/lib/annotations/context";
import {
  recordKey,
  resolveRecord,
  type ColumnGetter,
} from "@/lib/annotations/resolve";
import {
  STATISTIC_NAMES,
  type AnnotationPopulation,
  type AnnotationStatistic,
  type AnnotationYAxis,
  type RecordSetAnnotation,
  type RegionAnnotation,
  type BandAnnotation,
  type CalloutAnnotation,
  type ChartAnnotation,
  type IntervalAnnotation,
  type ReferenceLineAnnotation,
} from "@/lib/annotations/types";
import { FieldSelector } from "../../FieldSelector";
import { NumericInputEnter } from "../../NumericInputEnter";
import { Button } from "../../ui/button";
import { Label } from "../../ui/label";
import { Switch } from "../../ui/switch";
import { Choice, HeldInput, ValueInput } from "./annotationInputs";

export interface FieldsProps<T extends ChartAnnotation> {
  annotation: T;
  context: AnnotationContext;
  onChange: (next: T) => void;
}

const AXIS_OPTIONS = (context: AnnotationContext) => [
  {
    value: "y" as const,
    label: "Y value",
    help: "Across the plot at a Y value, such as a target or tolerance.",
  },
  {
    value: "x" as const,
    label: context.xDate ? "Date" : "X value",
    help: context.xDate
      ? "Up the plot at a date, such as a release or an event."
      : "Up the plot at an X value, such as a threshold.",
  },
];

/** Which rows a calculation or pick reads. */
function PopulationChoice({
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: AnnotationPopulation;
  onChange: (value: AnnotationPopulation) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Choice
      label="Rows read"
      value={value}
      onChange={onChange}
      options={[
        {
          value: "all",
          label: "All rows",
          help: "Every row the chart can draw. Selections in other charts don't change it.",
        },
        {
          value: "filtered",
          label: "Filtered rows",
          help: "Only rows that pass the current filters, so it updates as the selection changes.",
        },
      ]}
    />
  );
}

/** The vertical axis a value reads on, when the chart has a right axis. */
function YAxisChoice({
  context,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  context: AnnotationContext;
  value: AnnotationYAxis | undefined;
  onChange: (value: AnnotationYAxis | undefined) => void;
}) {
  if (!context.rightAxis) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Y axis</span>
      <Choice
        label="Y axis"
        value={value ?? "left"}
        onChange={(next) => onChange(next === "left" ? undefined : next)}
        options={[
          {
            value: "left",
            label: "Left",
            help: "Read Y values on the left axis.",
          },
          {
            value: "right",
            label: "Right",
            help: "Read Y values on the right axis, with the series drawn there.",
          },
        ]}
      />
    </>
  );
}

export function CalloutFields({
  annotation: callout,
  context,
  onChange,
  keyFields,
  getColumn,
  ids,
}: FieldsProps<CalloutAnnotation> & {
  keyFields: string[];
  getColumn: ColumnGetter;
  ids: IdType[];
}) {
  const attach = callout.attach;
  const toRecord = (field: string) => {
    // Keep the same record when switching key fields, if one resolves.
    const resolved =
      attach.kind === "record"
        ? resolveRecord(attach, getColumn, ids)
        : undefined;
    const key =
      resolved?.status === "found"
        ? recordKey([field], getColumn, resolved.id)
        : undefined;
    onChange({
      ...callout,
      attach: {
        kind: "record",
        keyFields: [field],
        key: key ?? [""],
        yField: attach.kind === "record" ? attach.yField : undefined,
      },
    });
  };
  const canAttachRecord = context.records && keyFields.length > 0;
  // Offer only attachments this chart can draw, plus the current one.
  const attachOptions = (
    [
      context.records && {
        value: "record" as const,
        label: "Record",
        help: keyFields.length
          ? "Follow one record, found by a key field, through filters and data updates."
          : "No field identifies every row once, so a record can't be attached.",
        disabled: !canAttachRecord && attach.kind !== "record",
      },
      context.categoryField && {
        value: "category" as const,
        label: "Category",
        help: "Attach to one bar by its category. A bar sums many rows, so it is never one record.",
      },
      context.plane && {
        value: "coordinate" as const,
        label: "Fixed point",
        help: "Stay at fixed X and Y values, whatever the rows do.",
      },
      context.records && {
        value: "query" as const,
        label: "Extreme",
        help: "Follow whichever row has the largest or smallest value. The row can change as data or filters change.",
      },
    ] as const
  ).filter((option): option is Exclude<typeof option, false | undefined | ""> =>
    Boolean(option)
  );
  return (
    <div className="eda-setting-grid">
      <Label htmlFor={`${callout.id}-text`}>Text</Label>
      <HeldInput
        id={`${callout.id}-text`}
        value={callout.text}
        placeholder="What this point shows"
        onChange={(text) => onChange({ ...callout, text })}
      />
      {attachOptions.length > 1 && (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Attach to</span>
          <Choice
            label="Attach to"
            value={attach.kind}
            onChange={(kind) => {
              if (kind === attach.kind) return;
              if (kind === "record") {
                if (keyFields[0]) toRecord(keyFields[0]);
              } else if (kind === "coordinate")
                onChange({
                  ...callout,
                  attach: { kind: "coordinate", x: 0, y: 0 },
                });
              else if (kind === "query")
                onChange({
                  ...callout,
                  attach: {
                    kind: "query",
                    pick: "max",
                    population: "filtered",
                  },
                });
              else
                onChange({
                  ...callout,
                  attach: { kind: "category", value: callout.text },
                });
            }}
            options={attachOptions}
          />
        </>
      )}
      {attach.kind === "category" ? (
        <>
          <Label htmlFor={`${callout.id}-category`}>Category</Label>
          <HeldInput
            id={`${callout.id}-category`}
            value={attach.value}
            placeholder="The bar's category, as labeled"
            onChange={(value) =>
              onChange({ ...callout, attach: { kind: "category", value } })
            }
          />
        </>
      ) : null}
      {attach.kind === "category" ? null : attach.kind === "query" ? (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Pick</span>
          <Choice
            label="Pick"
            value={attach.pick}
            onChange={(pick) =>
              onChange({ ...callout, attach: { ...attach, pick } })
            }
            options={[
              {
                value: "max",
                label: "Largest",
                help: "The row with the largest value.",
              },
              {
                value: "min",
                label: "Smallest",
                help: "The row with the smallest value.",
              },
            ]}
          />
          {context.measureFields.length > 1 && (
            <>
              <span className="eda-setting-label">Of</span>
              <FieldSelector
                label=""
                value={attach.field ?? context.measureFields[0]!}
                fields={context.measureFields}
                onChange={(field) =>
                  field &&
                  onChange({ ...callout, attach: { ...attach, field } })
                }
              />
            </>
          )}
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Among</span>
          <PopulationChoice
            value={attach.population}
            onChange={(population) =>
              onChange({ ...callout, attach: { ...attach, population } })
            }
          />
        </>
      ) : attach.kind === "record" ? (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Key field</span>
          <FieldSelector
            label=""
            placeholder="Field that identifies a row"
            value={attach.keyFields[0] ?? ""}
            fields={
              keyFields.includes(attach.keyFields[0] ?? "")
                ? keyFields
                : [...attach.keyFields, ...keyFields]
            }
            onChange={(field) => field && toRecord(field)}
          />
          <Label htmlFor={`${callout.id}-key`}>Key value</Label>
          <HeldInput
            id={`${callout.id}-key`}
            value={String(attach.key[0] ?? "")}
            placeholder="The record's value"
            onChange={(value) =>
              onChange({ ...callout, attach: { ...attach, key: [value] } })
            }
          />
          {context.measureFields.length > 1 && (
            <>
              <span className="eda-setting-label">On series</span>
              <FieldSelector
                label=""
                value={attach.yField ?? context.measureFields[0]!}
                fields={context.measureFields}
                onChange={(yField) =>
                  yField &&
                  onChange({ ...callout, attach: { ...attach, yField } })
                }
              />
            </>
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">
            {context.xDate ? "Date, Y" : "X, Y"}
          </span>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <ValueInput
              aria-label={context.xDate ? "Callout date" : "Callout X value"}
              date={context.xDate}
              value={attach.x}
              onChange={(x) =>
                onChange({ ...callout, attach: { ...attach, x } })
              }
            />
            <ValueInput
              aria-label="Callout Y value"
              value={attach.y}
              onChange={(y) =>
                onChange({ ...callout, attach: { ...attach, y } })
              }
            />
          </div>
          <YAxisChoice
            context={context}
            value={callout.yAxis}
            onChange={(yAxis) => onChange({ ...callout, yAxis })}
          />
        </>
      )}
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Label offset</span>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <NumericInputEnter
          aria-label="Label offset right, in pixels"
          value={callout.offset.dx}
          onChange={(dx) =>
            onChange({ ...callout, offset: { ...callout.offset, dx } })
          }
        />
        <NumericInputEnter
          aria-label="Label offset down, in pixels"
          value={callout.offset.dy}
          onChange={(dy) =>
            onChange({ ...callout, offset: { ...callout.offset, dy } })
          }
        />
      </div>
      <Label htmlFor={`${callout.id}-leader`}>Leader line</Label>
      <Switch
        id={`${callout.id}-leader`}
        checked={callout.leader !== false}
        onCheckedChange={(checked) =>
          onChange({ ...callout, leader: checked ? undefined : false })
        }
      />
      <Label htmlFor={`${callout.id}-arrow`}>Arrowhead</Label>
      <Switch
        id={`${callout.id}-arrow`}
        checked={Boolean(callout.arrow)}
        onCheckedChange={(checked) =>
          onChange({ ...callout, arrow: checked || undefined })
        }
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export function ReferenceFields({
  annotation: reference,
  context,
  onChange,
}: FieldsProps<ReferenceLineAnnotation>) {
  const date = context.xDate && reference.axis === "x";
  return (
    <div className="eda-setting-grid">
      {/* Charts of grouped values have one value axis. */}
      {context.plane && (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Axis</span>
          <Choice
            label="Reference axis"
            value={reference.axis}
            onChange={(axis) => onChange({ ...reference, axis })}
            options={AXIS_OPTIONS(context)}
          />
        </>
      )}
      {context.records && (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Value from</span>
          <Choice
            label="Value from"
            value={reference.calculated ? "calculated" : "fixed"}
            onChange={(kind) =>
              onChange({
                ...reference,
                calculated:
                  kind === "calculated"
                    ? { statistic: "mean", population: "all" }
                    : undefined,
              })
            }
            options={[
              {
                value: "fixed",
                label: "Fixed",
                help: "A value you type, which filters never move.",
              },
              {
                value: "calculated",
                label: "Calculated",
                help: "A statistic of this axis's field over the rows you choose. Its label shows the result.",
              },
            ]}
          />
        </>
      )}
      {reference.calculated ? (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Statistic</span>
          <Choice
            label="Statistic"
            value={reference.calculated.statistic}
            onChange={(statistic: AnnotationStatistic) =>
              onChange({
                ...reference,
                calculated: { ...reference.calculated!, statistic },
              })
            }
            options={(
              Object.keys(STATISTIC_NAMES) as AnnotationStatistic[]
            ).map((statistic) => ({
              value: statistic,
              label: { mean: "Mean", median: "Median", min: "Min", max: "Max" }[
                statistic
              ],
              help: `The ${STATISTIC_NAMES[statistic].toLowerCase()} of the field on this axis.`,
            }))}
          />
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Rows read</span>
          <PopulationChoice
            value={reference.calculated.population}
            onChange={(population) =>
              onChange({
                ...reference,
                calculated: { ...reference.calculated!, population },
              })
            }
          />
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Label htmlFor={`${reference.id}-value`}>
            {date ? "Date" : "Value"}
          </Label>
          <ValueInput
            id={`${reference.id}-value`}
            date={date}
            value={reference.value}
            onChange={(value) => onChange({ ...reference, value })}
          />
        </>
      )}
      <Label htmlFor={`${reference.id}-label`}>Label</Label>
      <HeldInput
        id={`${reference.id}-label`}
        value={reference.label ?? ""}
        placeholder={reference.axis === "x" ? "Event" : "Target"}
        onChange={(label) =>
          onChange({ ...reference, label: label || undefined })
        }
      />
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Line</span>
      <Choice
        label="Line style"
        value={reference.lineStyle ?? "dashed"}
        onChange={(lineStyle) =>
          onChange({
            ...reference,
            lineStyle: lineStyle === "dashed" ? undefined : lineStyle,
          })
        }
        options={[
          {
            value: "dashed",
            label: "Dashed",
            help: "A dashed line, which reads as a reference rather than data.",
          },
          { value: "solid", label: "Solid", help: "A solid line." },
        ]}
      />
      {reference.axis === "y" && (
        <YAxisChoice
          context={context}
          value={reference.yAxis}
          onChange={(yAxis) => onChange({ ...reference, yAxis })}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function BandFields({
  annotation: band,
  context,
  onChange,
}: FieldsProps<BandAnnotation>) {
  const date = context.xDate && band.axis === "x";
  return (
    <div className="eda-setting-grid">
      {/* Charts of grouped values have one value axis. */}
      {context.plane && (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">Axis</span>
          <Choice
            label="Band axis"
            value={band.axis}
            onChange={(axis) => onChange({ ...band, axis })}
            options={[
              {
                value: "y",
                label: "Y range",
                help: "A span of Y values, such as a tolerance or normal range.",
              },
              {
                value: "x",
                label: context.xDate ? "Period" : "X range",
                help: context.xDate
                  ? "A span of dates, such as a campaign or an outage."
                  : "A span of X values.",
              },
            ]}
          />
        </>
      )}
      <span className="eda-setting-label">From, to</span>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <ValueInput
          aria-label="Band start"
          date={date}
          value={band.from}
          onChange={(from) => onChange({ ...band, from })}
        />
        <ValueInput
          aria-label="Band end"
          date={date}
          value={band.to}
          onChange={(to) => onChange({ ...band, to })}
        />
      </div>
      <Label htmlFor={`${band.id}-label`}>Label</Label>
      <HeldInput
        id={`${band.id}-label`}
        value={band.label ?? ""}
        placeholder="What the span means"
        onChange={(label) => onChange({ ...band, label: label || undefined })}
      />
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Show as</span>
      <Choice
        label="Band display"
        value={band.display ?? "fill"}
        onChange={(display) =>
          onChange({
            ...band,
            display: display === "fill" ? undefined : display,
          })
        }
        options={[
          {
            value: "fill",
            label: "Shaded",
            help: "Shade the span across the plot.",
          },
          {
            value: "bracket",
            label: "Bracket",
            help: "Mark the span with a bracket along the plot edge, leaving the marks unshaded.",
          },
        ]}
      />
      {band.axis === "y" && (
        <YAxisChoice
          context={context}
          value={band.yAxis}
          onChange={(yAxis) => onChange({ ...band, yAxis })}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function IntervalFields({
  annotation: interval,
  context,
  onChange,
}: FieldsProps<IntervalAnnotation>) {
  return (
    <div className="eda-setting-grid">
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Lower bound</span>
      <FieldSelector
        label=""
        placeholder="Field with each row's lower bound"
        value={interval.lowerField}
        onChange={(lowerField) =>
          lowerField && onChange({ ...interval, lowerField })
        }
      />
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Upper bound</span>
      <FieldSelector
        label=""
        placeholder="Field with each row's upper bound"
        value={interval.upperField}
        onChange={(upperField) =>
          upperField && onChange({ ...interval, upperField })
        }
      />
      <Label htmlFor={`${interval.id}-meaning`}>Meaning</Label>
      <HeldInput
        id={`${interval.id}-meaning`}
        value={interval.meaning}
        placeholder="Such as: supplied 90% forecast range"
        onChange={(meaning) => onChange({ ...interval, meaning })}
      />
      <YAxisChoice
        context={context}
        value={interval.yAxis}
        onChange={(yAxis) => onChange({ ...interval, yAxis })}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export function RegionFields({
  annotation: region,
  context,
  onChange,
}: FieldsProps<RegionAnnotation>) {
  return (
    <div className="eda-setting-grid">
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Shape</span>
      <Choice
        label="Region shape"
        value={region.shape}
        onChange={(shape) => onChange({ ...region, shape })}
        options={[
          {
            value: "rect",
            label: "Rectangle",
            help: "A box between two X values and two Y values.",
          },
          {
            value: "ellipse",
            label: "Ellipse",
            help: "An ellipse inside the box between two X values and two Y values.",
          },
        ]}
      />
      <span className="eda-setting-label">
        {context.xDate ? "Dates" : "X from, to"}
      </span>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <ValueInput
          aria-label="Region X start"
          date={context.xDate}
          value={region.x0}
          onChange={(x0) => onChange({ ...region, x0 })}
        />
        <ValueInput
          aria-label="Region X end"
          date={context.xDate}
          value={region.x1}
          onChange={(x1) => onChange({ ...region, x1 })}
        />
      </div>
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Y from, to</span>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <ValueInput
          aria-label="Region Y start"
          value={region.y0}
          onChange={(y0) => onChange({ ...region, y0 })}
        />
        <ValueInput
          aria-label="Region Y end"
          value={region.y1}
          onChange={(y1) => onChange({ ...region, y1 })}
        />
      </div>
      <Label htmlFor={`${region.id}-label`}>Label</Label>
      <HeldInput
        id={`${region.id}-label`}
        value={region.label ?? ""}
        placeholder="What this region means"
        onChange={(label) => onChange({ ...region, label: label || undefined })}
      />
      <YAxisChoice
        context={context}
        value={region.yAxis}
        onChange={(yAxis) => onChange({ ...region, yAxis })}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export function RecordSetFields({
  annotation: set,
  context,
  onChange,
  onReplace,
  replaceHelp,
}: FieldsProps<RecordSetAnnotation> & {
  /** Replaces the members with the rows the filters select now. */
  onReplace?: () => void;
  replaceHelp: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="eda-setting-grid">
      <span className="eda-setting-label">Records</span>
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">
          {set.keys.length.toLocaleString()} by {set.keyFields.join(", ")}
        </span>
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          className="h-7"
          disabled={!onReplace}
          tooltip={replaceHelp}
          onClick={onReplace}
        >
          Use selection
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Label htmlFor={`${set.id}-label`}>Label</Label>
      <HeldInput
        id={`${set.id}-label`}
        value={set.label ?? ""}
        placeholder="What these records share"
        onChange={(label) => onChange({ ...set, label: label || undefined })}
      />
      {context.measureFields.length > 1 && (
        <>
          <span className="eda-setting-label">On series</span>
          <FieldSelector
            label=""
            value={set.yField ?? context.measureFields[0]!}
            fields={context.measureFields}
            onChange={(yField) => yField && onChange({ ...set, yField })}
          />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~text
import { Copy, Eye, EyeOff, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { useDataLayer, type IdType } from "@/providers/DataLayerProvider";
import type { ChartSettings } from "@/types/ChartTypes";
import { useAnnotationRows } from "../../charts/annotations/useAnnotationRows";
import { useAnnotationStatuses } from "../../charts/annotations/annotationStatus";
import { Button } from "../../ui/button";
import { Label } from "../../ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "../../ui/select";
import { groupFacetData } from "../../charts/FacetRelated/FacetContainer";
import { ActionTooltip } from "../../ui/tooltip";
import {
  annotationContext,
  type AnnotationContext,
} from "@/lib/annotations/context";
import {
  createBand,
  createCallout,
  createInterval,
  createRecordSet,
  createReferenceLine,
  createRegion,
  duplicateAnnotation,
} from "@/lib/annotations/create";
import {
  ANNOTATION_STATUS_TEXT,
  resolveRecord,
  uniqueKeyFields,
  type AnnotationStatus,
  type ColumnGetter,
} from "@/lib/annotations/resolve";
import {
  ANNOTATION_TYPE_NAMES,
  annotationLabel,
  type ChartAnnotation,
} from "@/lib/annotations/types";
import { HeldInput } from "./annotationInputs";
import {
  BandFields,
  CalloutFields,
  IntervalFields,
  RecordSetFields,
  ReferenceFields,
  RegionFields,
} from "./AnnotationFields";

const EVERY_PANEL = "__every";

/** More saved records than this would bloat every saved analysis. */
const MAX_SAVED_RECORDS = 500;

interface AnnotationsSettingsTabProps {
  settings: ChartSettings;
  onSettingChange: (key: string, value: unknown) => void;
}

/**
 * What rows and filters say about an annotation, for when no drawn panel
 * has reported on it, such as while the chart is off screen.
 */
function listStatus(
  annotation: ChartAnnotation,
  ids: IdType[],
  getColumn: ColumnGetter,
  visible: Set<IdType>
): AnnotationStatus {
  if (annotation.hidden) return "hidden";
  if (annotation.type === "interval")
    return annotation.lowerField && annotation.upperField ? "shown" : "invalid";
  if (annotation.type !== "callout" || annotation.attach.kind !== "record")
    return "shown";
  const resolution = resolveRecord(annotation.attach, getColumn, ids);
  if (resolution.status !== "found") return resolution.status;
  return visible.has(resolution.id) ? "shown" : "filtered";
}

export function AnnotationsSettingsTab({
  settings,
  onSettingChange,
}: AnnotationsSettingsTabProps) {
  const annotations = useMemo(
    () => settings.annotations ?? [],
    [settings.annotations]
  );
  const context = annotationContext(settings);
  // After a removal, focus moves to the card that took its place, or to
  // the add buttons, rather than falling back to the page.
  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const focusAfterRemove = useRef<number | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const index = focusAfterRemove.current;
    if (index === null) return;
    focusAfterRemove.current = null;
    const cards = rootRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
      "li[data-annotation-id]"
    );
    const card = cards?.[Math.min(index, (cards?.length ?? 1) - 1)];
    (
      card?.querySelector<HTMLElement>("button") ??
      rootRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>("button")
    )?.focus();
  }, [annotations]);
  const { ids, getColumn } = useAnnotationRows();
  const drawn = useAnnotationStatuses(settings.id);
  const getColumnNames = useDataLayer((state) => state.getColumnNames);
  const crossfilter = useDataLayer((state) => state.crossfilterWrapper);
  // Filter changes arrive as new live items for this chart.
  const liveItems = useDataLayer((state) => state.liveItems[settings.id]);
  const visible = useMemo(
    () => new Set(crossfilter.getChartFilteredRowIds(settings)),
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [crossfilter, liveItems, settings.filters, settings.localFilters]
  );
  const fields = getColumnNames();
  const keyFields = useMemo(
    () => uniqueKeyFields(fields, getColumn, ids),
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [fields.join("\u0000"), getColumn, ids]
  );
  const source = { fields, ids, getColumn, ...context };
  // Facet panels by the same ids the panels draw with, so a binding
  // follows its facet values rather than panel order.
  const facetOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const facet = settings.facet;
    if (!facet.enabled || !facet.rowVariable) return [];
    return groupFacetData(
      ids,
      getColumn(facet.rowVariable),
      facet.type === "grid" && facet.columnVariable
        ? getColumn(facet.columnVariable)
        : null
    ).map((group) => ({
      id: group.id,
      label: [group.rowValue, group.columnValue].filter(Boolean).join(" · "),
    }));
  }, [settings.facet, ids, getColumn]);

  const write = (next: ChartAnnotation[]) =>
    onSettingChange("annotations", next.length ? next : undefined);
  const add = (annotation: ChartAnnotation) =>
    write([...annotations, annotation]);
  // Saved records copy the rows the filters select now, by key.
  const selectionSize = visible.size;
  const selecting = selectionSize > 0 && selectionSize < ids.length;
  const canSave =
    context.records &&
    keyFields.length > 0 &&
    selecting &&
    selectionSize <= MAX_SAVED_RECORDS;
  const saveHelp = !context.records
    ? "This chart draws grouped values, not single records."
    : !keyFields.length
      ? "No field identifies every row once, so records can't be saved by key."
      : !selecting
        ? "Select rows first, by brushing or clicking marks in any chart. Saved records keep that membership after the selection changes."
        : selectionSize > MAX_SAVED_RECORDS
          ? `The selection has ${selectionSize.toLocaleString()} rows. Narrow it to ${MAX_SAVED_RECORDS} or fewer to save them.`
          : `Save the ${selectionSize.toLocaleString()} selected rows by key. Their membership stays fixed when filters change.`;
  const selectedRecords = () =>
    createRecordSet(keyFields[0]!, [...visible], getColumn);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2.5" ref={rootRef}>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        Annotations save with this chart. A callout on a record follows that
        record through filters and data updates.
      </p>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Add</span>
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          tooltip={
            context.records
              ? "Add a label attached to the record with the largest value. Change the record or text below."
              : context.categoryField
                ? "Add a label on the category with the most rows. Change the category or text below."
                : "Add a label at a fixed point. Change its position and text below."
          }
          onClick={() => add(createCallout(source))}
        >
          Callout
        </Button>
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          tooltip={
            context.xDate
              ? "Add a vertical line at a date, such as a release. Change its date below."
              : "Add a fixed target line at a typical value. Change its axis and value below."
          }
          onClick={() => add(createReferenceLine(source))}
        >
          Line
        </Button>
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          tooltip={
            context.xDate
              ? "Add a shaded period between two dates. Change its dates below."
              : "Add a shaded range between two values, such as a tolerance. Change it below."
          }
          onClick={() => add(createBand(source))}
        >
          Band
        </Button>
        {context.plane && (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            tooltip="Add a rectangle or ellipse in data units, which marks an area of the plot. Change its bounds below."
            onClick={() => add(createRegion(source))}
          >
            Region
          </Button>
        )}
        {context.records && (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            disabled={!canSave}
            tooltip={saveHelp}
            onClick={() => add(selectedRecords())}
          >
            Saved records
          </Button>
        )}
        {context.intervals && (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            tooltip="Shade between lower and upper bounds that each row already holds, such as a supplied forecast range. The chart computes no interval itself."
            onClick={() => add(createInterval(source))}
          >
            Interval
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      {annotations.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No annotations yet.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-2" aria-label="Annotations">
          {annotations.map((annotation, index) => (
            <AnnotationCard
              key={annotation.id}
              annotation={annotation}
              status={
                annotation.hidden
                  ? "hidden"
                  : (drawn[annotation.id] ??
                    listStatus(annotation, ids, getColumn, visible))
              }
              context={context}
              facetOptions={facetOptions}
              keyFields={keyFields}
              getColumn={getColumn}
              ids={ids}
              onChange={(next) =>
                write(
                  annotations.map((item) => (item.id === next.id ? next : item))
                )
              }
              onReplaceRecords={
                canSave && annotation.type === "record-set"
                  ? () =>
                      write(
                        annotations.map((item) =>
                          item.id === annotation.id
                            ? {
                                ...selectedRecords(),
                                id: item.id,
                                label: annotation.label,
                                name: annotation.name,
                              }
                            : item
                        )
                      )
                  : undefined
              }
              replaceHelp={saveHelp}
              onDuplicate={() =>
                write([
                  ...annotations.slice(0, index + 1),
                  duplicateAnnotation(annotation),
                  ...annotations.slice(index + 1),
                ])
              }
              onRemove={() => {
                focusAfterRemove.current = index;
                write(annotations.filter((item) => item.id !== annotation.id));
              }}
            />
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function AnnotationCard({
  annotation,
  status,
  context,
  facetOptions,
  keyFields,
  getColumn,
  ids,
  onChange,
  onReplaceRecords,
  replaceHelp,
  onDuplicate,
  onRemove,
}: {
  annotation: ChartAnnotation;
  status: AnnotationStatus;
  context: AnnotationContext;
  facetOptions: { id: string; label: string }[];
  keyFields: string[];
  getColumn: ColumnGetter;
  ids: IdType[];
  onChange: (next: ChartAnnotation) => void;
  onReplaceRecords?: () => void;
  replaceHelp: string;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onRemove: () => void;
}) {
  const label = annotationLabel(annotation);
  const statusText =
    status === "shown" ? undefined : ANNOTATION_STATUS_TEXT[status];
  const fieldProps = { context, onChange };
  return (
    <li
      className="space-y-2 rounded-md border border-border p-2"
      data-annotation-id={annotation.id}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs font-medium">
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">
            {ANNOTATION_TYPE_NAMES[annotation.type]}:{" "}
          </span>
          {label}
        </span>
        {statusText && (
          <ActionTooltip content={statusText.help}>
            <span
              className={`shrink-0 rounded-sm px-1 text-[11px] ${
                status === "hidden" ||
                status === "filtered" ||
                status === "offscreen"
                  ? "text-muted-foreground"
                  : "text-warning"
              }`}
              tabIndex={0}
              role="status"
            >
              {statusText.label}
            </span>
          </ActionTooltip>
        )}
        <Button
          size="icon"
          variant="ghost"
          className="size-6"
          aria-label={annotation.hidden ? `Show ${label}` : `Hide ${label}`}
          tooltip={
            annotation.hidden
              ? "Show this annotation on the chart"
              : "Hide this annotation without removing it"
          }
          onClick={() =>
            onChange({
              ...annotation,
              hidden: annotation.hidden ? undefined : true,
            })
          }
        >
          {annotation.hidden ? (
            <EyeOff className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : (
            <Eye className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
        </Button>
        <Button
          size="icon"
          variant="ghost"
          className="size-6"
          aria-label={`Duplicate ${label}`}
          tooltip="Add a copy of this annotation below it"
          onClick={onDuplicate}
        >
          <Copy className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          size="icon"
          variant="ghost"
          className="size-6"
          aria-label={`Remove ${label}`}
          tooltip="Remove this annotation"
          onClick={onRemove}
        >
          <Trash2 className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div className="eda-setting-grid">
        <Label htmlFor={`${annotation.id}-name`}>Name</Label>
        <HeldInput
          id={`${annotation.id}-name`}
          value={annotation.name ?? ""}
          placeholder={label}
          onChange={(name) =>
            onChange({ ...annotation, name: name || undefined })
          }
        />
        {facetOptions.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <span className="eda-setting-label">Panel</span>
            <Select
              value={annotation.facet ?? EVERY_PANEL}
              onValueChange={(facet) =>
                onChange({
                  ...annotation,
                  facet: facet === EVERY_PANEL ? undefined : facet,
                })
              }
            >
              <ActionTooltip content="Draw it in every facet panel, or only in the panel for one set of facet values.">
                <SelectTrigger aria-label="Facet panel" className="h-8 w-full">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
              </ActionTooltip>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value={EVERY_PANEL}>Every panel</SelectItem>
                {facetOptions.map((option) => (
                  <SelectItem key={option.id} value={option.id}>
                    {option.label}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
                {annotation.facet &&
                  !facetOptions.some(
                    (option) => option.id === annotation.facet
                  ) && (
                    <SelectItem value={annotation.facet}>
                      A panel with no rows now
                    </SelectItem>
                  )}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      {annotation.type === "callout" ? (
        <CalloutFields
          annotation={annotation}
          {...fieldProps}
          keyFields={keyFields}
          getColumn={getColumn}
          ids={ids}
        />
      ) : annotation.type === "reference-line" ? (
        <ReferenceFields annotation={annotation} {...fieldProps} />
      ) : annotation.type === "band" ? (
        <BandFields annotation={annotation} {...fieldProps} />
      ) : annotation.type === "region" ? (
        <RegionFields annotation={annotation} {...fieldProps} />
      ) : annotation.type === "record-set" ? (
        <RecordSetFields
          annotation={annotation}
          {...fieldProps}
          onReplace={onReplaceRecords}
          replaceHelp={replaceHelp}
        />
      ) : (
        <IntervalFields annotation={annotation} {...fieldProps} />
      )}
    </li>
  );
}
~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~text
import { useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useDataLayer } from "@/providers/DataLayerProvider";
import { parseDateText } from "@/lib/valueParsing";
import { NumericInputEnter } from "../../NumericInputEnter";
import { Input } from "../../ui/input";
import { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem } from "../../ui/toggle-group";
import { ActionTooltip } from "../../ui/tooltip";

export const toggleClass =
  "w-full min-w-0 px-1 text-xs aria-checked:bg-primary/10 aria-checked:font-semibold aria-checked:text-primary";

/**
 * Typing in a field is one host change, reported when the field loses
 * focus, rather than one undo step per keystroke.
 */
export function useHeldTyping() {
  const holdStateChanges = useDataLayer((state) => state.holdStateChanges);
  const release = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null);
  return {
    onFocus: () => {
      release.current ??= holdStateChanges();
    },
    onBlur: () => {
      release.current?.();
      release.current = null;
    },
  };
}

/** A text field whose typing is one undo step. */
export function HeldInput({
  id,
  value,
  placeholder,
  onChange,
  "aria-label": ariaLabel,
}: {
  id?: string;
  value: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  "aria-label"?: string;
}) {
  const typing = useHeldTyping();
  return (
    <Input
      id={id}
      value={value}
      placeholder={placeholder}
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      {...typing}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
    />
  );
}

const dateText = (time: number) =>
  Number.isFinite(time) ? new Date(time).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : "";

/**
 * A value in data units: a number, or a calendar date on a date axis, which
 * saves as a UTC timestamp.
 */
export function ValueInput({
  id,
  value,
  date,
  onChange,
  "aria-label": ariaLabel,
}: {
  id?: string;
  value: number;
  date?: boolean;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
  "aria-label"?: string;
}) {
  if (date)
    return (
      <Input
        id={id}
        type="date"
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        value={dateText(value)}
        onChange={(event) => {
          const time = parseDateText(event.target.value);
          if (Number.isFinite(time)) onChange(time);
        }}
      />
    );
  return (
    <NumericInputEnter
      id={id}
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      className="max-w-none"
      value={value}
      onChange={onChange}
    />
  );
}

/** A two-way choice, each option explained by a tooltip. */
export function Choice<T extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: T;
  options: { value: T; label: string; help: ReactNode; disabled?: boolean }[];
  onChange: (value: T) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <ToggleGroup
      type="single"
      value={value}
      onValueChange={(next) => {
        if (next) onChange(next as T);
      }}
      aria-label={label}
      variant="outline"
      size="sm"
      className="grid w-full"
      style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${options.length}, 1fr)` }}
    >
      {options.map((option) => (
        <ActionTooltip key={option.value} content={option.help}>
          <ToggleGroupItem
            value={option.value}
            aria-label={option.label}
            className={toggleClass}
            disabled={option.disabled}
          >
            {option.label}
          </ToggleGroupItem>
        </ActionTooltip>
      ))}
    </ToggleGroup>
  );
}
~~~~~~~~

## Source: packages/explorEDA/src/hooks/useColorScales.ts

~~~~~~~~text
import { finiteNumber } from "@/lib/valueParsing";
import { detectColumnType } from "@/components/SummaryTable/utils/dataTypeDetection";
import {
  useDataLayer,
  useDataLayerSnapshot,
} from "@/providers/DataLayerProvider";
import {
  CategoricalColorScale,
  ColorScaleType,
  NumericalColorScale,
  UseColorScalesReturn,
} from "@/types/ColorScaleTypes";
import type { datum } from "@/types/ChartTypes";
import { interpolateViridis } from "d3-scale-chromatic";
import { scaleSequential } from "d3-scale";
import type { ScaleOrdinal, ScaleSequential } from "d3-scale";
import {
  assignCategoryColors,
  defaultColorScaleForField,
  makeColorScale,
  makeD3ColorScale,
} from "@/lib/colorScaleMath";
import { DEFAULT_SEQUENTIAL_PALETTE } from "@/lib/colorPalettes";

import { useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import { useDisplayColorScales } from "./useDisplayColorScales";
import { THEME_PALETTE } from "@/lib/themePalettes";

export function useColorScales(): UseColorScalesReturn {
  // Charts and editors see colors as drawn in the current theme and mode.
  const colorScales = useDisplayColorScales();
  const addColorScale = useDataLayer((state) => state.addColorScale);
  const removeColorScale = useDataLayer((state) => state.removeColorScale);
  const updateColorScale = useDataLayer((state) => state.updateColorScale);
  const getColumnData = useDataLayer((state) => state.getColumnData);
  const fieldProfiles = useDataLayer((state) => state.fieldProfiles);
  // Read when a scale is chosen; a subscription would re-render every chart.
  const getState = useDataLayerSnapshot();

  // One color function per scale, shared with the chart plans.
  const colorFunctions = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Map(colorScales.map((scale) => [scale.id, makeColorScale(scale)])),
    [colorScales]
  );
  const d3Scales = useMemo(() => {
    const scales = new Map<
      string,
      ScaleSequential<string> | ScaleOrdinal<string, string>
    >();

    colorScales.forEach((scale) =>
      scales.set(scale.id, makeD3ColorScale(scale))
    );

    return scales;
  }, [colorScales]);

  const getColorForValue = useCallback(
    (
      scaleId: string | undefined,
      value: datum,
      defaultColor: string = "#000000"
    ): string => {
      if (!scaleId) {
        return defaultColor;
      }

      const color = colorFunctions.get(scaleId);
      if (!color) {
        return "#000000";
      }

      try {
        return color(value);
      } catch {
        return "#000000";
      }
    },
    [colorFunctions]
  );

  const getScaleById = (id: string): ColorScaleType | undefined => {
    return colorScales.find((s) => s.id === id);
  };

  const createDefaultNumericalScale = (
    name: string,
    min: number,
    max: number,
    sourceField?: string
  ): ColorScaleType => {
    const scale: Omit<NumericalColorScale, "id"> = {
      name,
      type: "numerical",
      palette: DEFAULT_SEQUENTIAL_PALETTE,
      min,
      max,
      sourceField,
    };
    return addColorScale(scale);
  };

  const createDefaultCategoricalScale = (
    name: string,
    values: string[],
    sourceField?: string,
    counts?: ReadonlyMap<string, number>
  ): ColorScaleType => {
    // The largest groups take the most distinct colors; the smallest past the
    // palette's end share a neutral gray.
    const assignment = {
      paletteId: THEME_PALETTE,
      order: counts ? ("frequency" as const) : ("data" as const),
      overflow: "other" as const,
    };
    const { mapping, palette } = assignCategoryColors(
      values,
      assignment,
      counts
    );

    const scale: Omit<CategoricalColorScale, "id"> = {
      name,
      type: "categorical",
      palette,
      mapping,
      sourceField,
      ...assignment,
    };
    return addColorScale(scale);
  };

  const getD3Scale = (scaleId: string) => {
    return (
      d3Scales.get(scaleId) ??
      scaleSequential(interpolateViridis).domain([0, 1])
    );
  };

  const getOrCreateScaleForField = (field: string, name?: string): string => {
    // Existing chart bindings are authoritative. Keep the exact ID instead of
    // matching display names.
    const boundScaleIds = new Set(
      getState()
        .charts.filter(
          (chart) =>
            chart.colorField === field && typeof chart.colorScaleId === "string"
        )
        .map((chart) => chart.colorScaleId as string)
    );
    if (boundScaleIds.size === 1) {
      const [boundScaleId] = boundScaleIds;
      if (
        boundScaleId &&
        colorScales.some((scale) => scale.id === boundScaleId)
      ) {
        return boundScaleId;
      }
    }

    // Otherwise reuse the explicitly bound source scale before creating one.
    const existingScale = colorScales.find((s) => s.sourceField === field);
    if (existingScale) {
      return existingScale.id;
    }

    // Get all values for the field
    const values = Object.values(getColumnData(field));
    const cleanValues = values.filter((v): v is string | number => v != null);

    // Check if values are numerical
    const profile = fieldProfiles.find((item) => item.name === field);
    const isNumerical =
      (profile?.dataType ?? detectColumnType(getColumnData(field))) ===
        "numeric" &&
      cleanValues.some((value) => finiteNumber(value) !== undefined);

    const newScale = addColorScale(
      defaultColorScaleForField(
        field,
        isNumerical ? cleanValues : values,
        isNumerical,
        name
      )
    );

    return newScale.id;
  };

  return {
    addColorScale,
    removeColorScale,
    updateColorScale,
    getColorForValue,
    getScaleById,
    colorScales,
    createDefaultNumericalScale,
    createDefaultCategoricalScale,
    getD3Scale,
    getOrCreateScaleForField,
  };
}
~~~~~~~~
